- **subagent-brief**: Use BEFORE invoking the Task or Agent tool to spawn a subagent. Anthropic does NOT share prefix cache across subagents — every subagent cold-starts and re-tokenizes its full prompt (system prompt + tool definitions + the context you handed it). Spawning N subagents with full context = N× token cost; a single fan-out can burn an entire Max-plan day. This skill enforces a pre-flight discipline: compress every subagent prompt into a ≤200-word brief before spawning.
